Conclusion: 通过面部关键点引导的空间增强与全局关键点补偿，该方法有效弥补了语音特征与面部空间布局之间的信息鸿沟，显著提升了音频驱动数字人的面部表现质量与唇音同步效果。

Abstract: Audio-driven digital human generation plays an important role in virtual communication, immersive interaction, and media production. With the development of Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), recent talking-head systems have obtained more faithful 3D facial geometry and appearance modeling. A remaining difficulty is that speech features mainly describe temporal acoustic patterns rather than explicit facial layouts. As a result, directly driving 3D facial deformation with audio may produce inaccurate mouth motion, weak expression details, and local artifacts. To address this issue, we propose a facial-keypoint-guided spatial enhancement module. The predicted landmarks provide structural cues for selecting and enriching spatial points around expression-sensitive facial regions. We further introduce a global landmark compensation mechanism, where the full set of keypoints is encoded into a conditioning vector to refine 3DGS attributes. This compensation supplies whole-face structural information to the underlying shape representation. Experiments under self-driven and cross-driven settings show that the proposed method improves visual quality, facial realism, and lip synchronization.

</details>


### [237] [JustFit: 200K-Token LLM Serving on a 24 GiB Laptop with Just-in-Time State Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17475)
*Yuhua Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JustFit 是一个基于 MLX 的本地推理运行时，通过 KVExec（压缩 KV 执行）、PhaseSwap（组件驻留管理）和 StateTrans（状态保持的切换）三大机制，在 24GiB 内存的 M4 Pro MacBook 上将 Qwen3.8-27B MXFP4 的单请求上下文从 30,720 提升至 212,992 位置（6.93 倍），并在 AIME 2026 上取得 29/30 的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源的开放权重模型使本地编码与推理变得有吸引力，但其上下文和执行状态会占用大量笔记本内存，限制了本地服务的上下文长度与并发能力。

Method: 提出基于 MLX 的推理运行时 JustFit，融合三套机制：KVExec 负责压缩 KV 的执行；PhaseSwap 管理组件驻留（component residency）；StateTrans 支持保留状态的 serving 切换。三者协同实现重构与即时（just-in-time）物化/释放的协调，且独立于模型权重量化方式。

Result: 在 24 GiB M4 Pro MacBook 上运行 Qwen3.8-27B MXFP4 的全执行容量测试中，三次独立运行各完成 196,608 输入与 16,384 输出 token，将单个请求可完成的上下文从 mlx-vlm 基线的 30,720 位置提升到 212,992（6.93 倍）；另一次双请求运行聚合保留 229,376 位置。性能测试中，32K 输入 / 64 输出的探针达到 19.11 tokens/s，重复的 32K+6K 工作负载中位峰值进程占用为 16,374 MiB。该运行时在 AIME 2026 上答对 29/30 题。

Conclusion: 紧凑的状态与生命周期感知的执行能够在支持扩展生成推理的同时，显著扩展本地服务的容量，展示了 JustFit 在受限内存设备上提升本地大模型服务能力的有效性。

Abstract: Capable open-weight models make local coding and reasoning attractive, but their context and execution state strain laptop memory. We present JustFit, an MLX-based inference runtime that combines KVExec for compressed KV execution, PhaseSwap for component residency, and StateTrans for state-preserving serving transitions. These mechanisms fuse reconstruction and coordinate just-in-time materialization and release, independently of model-weight quantization. In full-execution capacity tests on a 24 GiB M4 Pro MacBook running Qwen3.8-27B MXFP4, three independent runs complete 196,608 input and 16,384 output tokens, increasing completed single-request context from the mlx-vlm baseline's 30,720 positions to 212,992 (6.93x); a separate two-request run retains 229,376 positions in aggregate. In separate performance tests, a 32K-input, 64-output probe reaches 19.11 tokens/s, and a repeated 32K+6K workload has a median peak process footprint of 16,374 MiB. The integrated runtime answers 29 of 30 AIME 2026 problems correctly, showing how compact state and lifetime-aware execution expand local serving capacity while supporting extended generated reasoning.

</details>


### [238] [LimiX-2: A Contextual Mechanism Network Towards General Structured-Data Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17488)
*Xingxuan Zhang,Gang Ren,Hao Yuan,Hao Zou,Hongze Tan,Hui Wang,Jianhao Song,Jiansheng Li,Jiayao Zhang,Jinghan Zhang,Kaifang Li,Lang Mo,Li Mao,Mingchao Hao,Nuo Xu,Rui Ding,Ruiji Zhang,Shuyang Li,Siyu Mei,Tianyang Zhang,Weiyang Mu,Yancheng Dong,Yongxian Wei,Yuan Xue,Yuanrui Wang,Yue He,Zijia Yang,Ziyun Li,Dongzhe Li,Fuqiang Wang,Jiandong Liu,Jiawei Chen,Jiaxin Du,Kaijie Cheng,Kehan Li,Lei Sun,Linjun Zhou,Ningbo Dai,Qi Wang,Renzhe Xu,Shaoxing Du,Shumeng Yang,Wang Lu,Wenjing Chu,Xiannan Huang,Xiaoyu Lin,Xing Ai,Xinyan Han,Xuanyue Li,Xuanyue Su,Xukun Zhang,Yan Lu,Yaxin Zhang,Yi Qin,Yifei Huang,Yihan Xu,Yongle Lv,Yuanyuan Jiang,Yushan Han,Peng Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LimiX-2 是 LimiX 家族的新模型，基于此前确立的缩放定律进行模型与数据扩展，采用上下文机制网络（CMNs）范式，并以上下文条件掩码建模（CCMM）预训练。它把表格数据的上下文学习目标从预测导向改为机制导向的联合建模，并在 TabArena、TALENT、BCCO 上超越现有数据集专用模型与表格基础模型，同时其特征注意力能编码直接因果关系，支持因果骨架恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动表格数据基础模型从"目标中心预测"（target-centric prediction，即传统表格 PFN 的 p(y|x, D_context)）转向"机制导向联合建模"（mechanism-oriented joint modeling，即 p(x, y|D_context)），以更好地捕捉数据生成的联合结构，并依据既定缩放定律进行规模化扩展。

Method: 采用上下文机制网络（Contextual Mechanism Networks, CMN）范式，围绕学习上下文依赖的联合结构 p(x, y|D_context) 设计网络；预训练采用上下文条件掩码建模（CCMM）；训练数据由结构因果模型（SCMs）合成的数据集构成，涵盖多样的图结构、功能机制和观测过程。

Result: 在 TabArena、TALENT 和 BCCO 三个基准上，LimiX-2 优于当前的数据集专用模型和表格基础模型；此外，其特征注意力能够编码直接因果关系，从而实现准确的因果骨架恢复。

Conclusion: 通过将上下文学习组织原则从目标中心预测转向机制导向联合建模，并结合模型与数据缩放，LimiX-2 不仅取得了领先的预测性能，还展现出因果感知能力，为表格基础模型开辟了兼顾预测与因果理解的新方向。

Abstract: We introduce LimiX-2, a new model in the LimiX family, developed through model and data scaling guided by our previously established scaling laws. LimiX-2 adopts the Contextual Mechanism Networks (CMNs) paradigm and is pretrained with Context-Conditional Masked Modeling (CCMM). CMNs shifts the organizing principle of in-context learning from target-centric prediction to mechanism-oriented joint modeling. Rather than centering the network on the $p(y \mid x, D_{\mathrm{context}})$ objective of conventional tabular PFNs, it is designed around learning $p(x, y \mid D_{\mathrm{context}})$, a context-dependent representation of the joint structure underlying data generation. Pretraining uses synthetic datasets generated by structural causal models (SCMs) spanning diverse graph structures, functional mechanisms, and observation processes. Evaluations on TabArena, TALENT, and BCCO show that LimiX-2 outperforms current dataset-specific models and tabular foundation models. Beyond predictive performance, the CMN paradigm also promotes causal awareness in LimiX-2: its feature attention encodes direct causal relationships, enabling accurate causal skeleton recovery.

</details>


### [239] [Verifiable Social Reasoning for LLM Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17496)
*Amir Taubenfeld,Zorik Gekhman,Avigail Grinstein-Dabush,Itay Laish,Ariel Goldstein,Marian Croak,Avinatan Hassidim,Yossi Matias,Amir Feder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Fuse，一个多智能体仿真框架，用于在用户介导的咨询场景中评估 LLM 的社会推理能力，并通过构造提供可验证的真值；对 12 个 LLM 的实验揭示了用户中介、偏见框架、信息需求与会话长度等因素的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估 LLM 助手在社会咨询场景中的社会推理能力存在两大难题：需要助手从主观的用户叙述中了解社会情境；以及社会属性（如他人的意图）通常缺乏可验证的真值。现有评估方法难以同时满足这两点。

Method: 提出 Fuse 多智能体仿真框架：一个具有隐藏动机的目标智能体与其他智能体（其中包括代表用户的智能体）互动，用户随后向被评估的 LLM 助手咨询以推断目标的动机，从而在构造上提供可验证的真值。仿真真实性通过包含 24k 标注的人类研究进行验证。

Result: 在 12 个 LLM 上应用 Fuse，并系统隔离关键因素，发现：(i) 用户中介加剧了社会推理固有的难度；(ii) LLM 对用户的有偏框架表现出系统性敏感性；(iii) 模型可能需要比人类更多的细节才能做出正确预测；(iv) 会话更长并不总能提升性能，尽管提供了澄清提问的机会。

Conclusion: Fuse 为研究用户介导的社会推理提供了可靠的评估平台，揭示了 LLM 在该场景下的关键局限。作者开源了 Fuse 及包含 21k 样本的数据集。

Abstract: LLM assistants are widely used for daily social advice, yet evaluating their social reasoning in such consultation settings remains challenging since (i) it requires setups where the assistant learns about social situations from subjective user narratives, and (ii) social properties, such as others' intentions, typically lack verifiable ground truth. To address these challenges, we introduce Fuse, a multi-agent simulation framework for studying user-mediated social reasoning. In Fuse, a target agent with a hidden motive interacts with other agents including one representing the user, who then consults the evaluated assistant to infer the target's motive, providing verifiable ground truth by construction. Simulation faithfulness is validated through a human study with 24k annotations. We apply Fuse to 12 LLMs and demonstrate its analytical utility by systematically isolating key factors, showing that (i) user mediation compounds the inherent difficulty of social reasoning; (ii) LLMs exhibit systematic sensitivity to biased user framing; (iii) models can require more details than humans need to reach a correct prediction; and (iv) longer conversations do not always improve performance despite providing opportunities for clarifying questions. We open-source Fuse and a dataset with 21k examples.

</details>


### [240] [ScienceBuddy: Recursive-in-Recursive Self-Improvement for Interactive Scientific Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17523)
*Shuhan Xue,Jianyuan Zhong,Ziyuan Nan,Wenbin Li,Zhaochen Yu,Jinchao Ding,Qiang Gao,Pengyu Zhan,Yuntong Zhang,Tian Cheng,Zhenfei Yin,Yingcheng Wu,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发布 ScienceBuddy，一个将可自我持续改进的科学智能体融入科研日常流程的交互式科研工作台，其核心是“递归套递归”的自改进范式，实现工具链演化与模型强化学习的耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科研工作日益复杂，现有科学智能体多为静态、一次性训练，无法在研究者持续使用中不断进步。作者希望打造一个能在真实科研协作中持续学习、与科研同步演化的“发现智能”（discovery intelligence）。

Method: 提出递归套递归（recursive-in-recursive）自改进范式：内层递归在模型固定的条件下改进工具链（harness），将研究者的请求、反馈与执行证据转化为任务和评估标准；外层递归在改进后的工具链上对模型进行强化学习训练。工具链演化塑造训练经验，模型学习又为工具链调整创造新机会，二者相互促进。

Result: 通过研究者交互、工具链细化与模型学习的案例研究，证明该范式可行，基准案例覆盖四类科学任务；并发布 ScienceBuddy 作为可用的研究产品。

Conclusion: ScienceBuddy 把持续改进的科学智能体与日常科研工作流结合，为迈向“发现智能”迈出一步，使科学 AI 能在与研究者的持续协作中演进，并随其所支持的研究同步进化。

Abstract: We introduce and release ScienceBuddy, an interactive scientific research workspace that brings continually improving scientific agents into researchers' everyday workflows. ScienceBuddy supports researchers in carrying out scientific tasks while transforming their requests, feedback, and execution evidence into tasks and evaluation rubrics for continual learning. At its core is recursive-in-recursive self-improvement, a paradigm that couples harness evolution with model reinforcement learning: the inner recursion improves the harness with the model fixed, while the outer recursion trains the model under the improved harness. Harness evolution shapes training experience, and model learning creates new opportunities for harness adaptation. We present case studies of researcher interaction, harness refinement, and model learning, with the benchmark cases spanning four scientific task families. By releasing ScienceBuddy as a research product, we make this paradigm available to the scientific community and take a step toward discovery intelligence: scientific AI that advances through sustained collaboration with researchers and evolves alongside the research it supports. Website: http://science-buddy.io

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [241] [EMODY Flow: Emotion-Aware Audio-Driven Full-Body Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16011)
*Harsh Kumar Agarwal,Xavier Alameda-Pineda,Olivier Perrotin*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出 EMODY Flow——一个约3500万参数的轻量流匹配框架，挂接在冻结的 Qwen-3 Omni 模型上，利用其内部 Mimi 音频编解码器并行条件化两个 DiT 生成器（SMPL-X 身体姿态与 FLAME 面部表情），并通过训练期辅助情感分类器恢复情感敏感性，在 BEAT2 上达到新 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身对话代理需要与语音和情感状态同步的全身运动，但全能大语言模型虽擅长多模态理解却只输出语言，存在具身响应生成的空白。核心问题在于情感条件失效：在同时给定丰富音频嵌入和离散情感标签时，流匹配模型往往抑制弱条件信号（情感标签），生成与指定情感几乎无关的运动。

Method: 提出 EMODY Flow 流匹配框架：冻结 Qwen-3 Omni 模型并复用其内部 Mimi 音频编解码器，作为两个并行 DiT 生成器（SMPL-X 身体姿态生成器、FLAME 面部表情生成器）的条件输入。训练时引入辅助情感分类器，强制生成的运动会可被情感识别，从而恢复情感敏感性。

Result: 在 BEAT2 手势质量上取得新 SOTA：FGD 0.302、Beat Correlation 0.853、Diversity 24.62，较此前最佳结果分别提升 26%、5%、62%；并在 TFHP 上实现零样本面部动画迁移，无需领域特定微调。通过 MDS 分析定性展示生成手势已实现情感分离。

Conclusion: EMODY Flow 以轻量流匹配框架解决了流匹配模型对弱情感条件信号抑制的问题，通过训练期辅助情感分类器恢复情感敏感性，在保持高质量手势生成的同时实现情感分离与零样本面部动画迁移，推进了具身对话代理的全身运动生成研究。

Abstract: Embodied conversational agents require synchronized full-body motion (body gestures and facial expressions) that aligns with speech and emotional state. Omni-modal large language models excel at multimodal understanding but produce only linguistic outputs, leaving a critical gap in embodied response generation. We identify and address a failure of emotion conditioning: like other conditional generators that under-use weak conditioning signals, a flow-matching model given both a rich audio embedding and a discrete emotion label suppresses the emotion, generating near-identical motion regardless of the specified emotion. We present EMODY Flow, a lightweight (around 35M parameters) flow-matching framework that attaches to a frozen Qwen-3 Omni model and reuses its internal Mimi audio-codecs to condition two parallel DiT generators - one for SMPL-X body pose, one for FLAME facial expressions. A training-time auxiliary emotion classifier restores emotion sensitivity by forcing generated motion to be emotion-identifiable. EMODY Flow sets a new state of the art on BEAT2 gesture quality, with FGD 0.302, Beat Correlation 0.853, and Diversity 24.62 - improving over the best prior results by 26%, 5%, and 62% respectively - and transfers to zero-shot facial animation on TFHP without domain-specific fine-tuning. Beyond these quantitative gains, the classifier yields clearly emotion-separated motion, which we demonstrate qualitatively through a multidimensional-scaling analysis of the generated gestures.

</details>


### [242] [Cascaded Non-Line-of-Sight Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16017)
*Diego Royo,María Peña,Forrest B. Peterson,Andreas Velten,Julio Marco,Diego Gutierrez*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种级联式非视距（NLOS）成像方法，利用高阶（四、五次）反弹光子信息，可对单个及多拐角场景中的隐藏物体成像，并在仿真与真实原型上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有飞行时间NLOS成像方法大多简单假设光子仅经历三次反弹路径，忽略了四、五次等高阶反弹光子中蕴含的有用信息，导致成像范围与场景适应性受限（如无法处理多拐角、不利朝向的目标）。

Method: 结合超快激光扫描与时间门控2D传感器阵列，在可见中继墙上捕获场景的冲激响应；由此计算任意其他隐藏墙处的等效虚拟冲激响应，从而级联第二个虚拟NLOS成像系统以利用高阶照明；同时分析了波基NLOS成像与粗糙隐藏墙的相互作用，并利用多面隐藏墙实现多视角成像。

Result: 在仿真和真实原型中验证了级联成像方法，实现了对困难朝向及隐藏于两个拐角后物体的四、五次反弹照明成像；解释了粗糙隐藏墙导致的可见性限制并加以克服；通过多面隐藏墙从不同视角成像，观察到此前不可见的特征。

Conclusion: 利用高阶反弹信息并级联虚拟NLOS成像系统，可显著扩展NLOS成像的场景范围与灵活性，突破传统三反弹假设的限制，为复杂隐藏场景成像提供了新途径。

Abstract: Time-of-flight non-line-of-sight (NLOS) imaging recovers information from hidden objects by analyzing the time of flight of indirect photons scattered on a visible (relay) wall. Most methods make the simplifying assumption that photons travel exclusively three-bounce paths, thus ignoring other useful information encoded in higher-order photons (with, e.g., four- or five-bounce paths). We present a novel cascaded NLOS imaging approach that leverages higher-order information and allows imaging a broader range of single- and multi-corner scenarios. We combine ultra-fast laser scanning with recent time-gated 2D sensor arrays to capture the scene's impulse response on a visible relay wall. From the captured impulse response, our method computes an analogous virtual impulse response at any other hidden wall. This effectively allows us to concatenate a second, virtual NLOS imaging system that leverages higher-order illumination. We validate our cascaded imaging method both in simulation and with a real prototype, demonstrating NLOS imaging with fourth- and fifth-bounce illumination of objects in challenging orientations and hidden around two corners. We also analyze how wave-based NLOS imaging interacts with rough hidden walls, which explains and helps overcome existing visibility limitations. We further illustrate how to image hidden objects from different perspectives, thus observing previously unseen features, by relying on multiple hidden walls.

</details>


### [243] [3D Field Data Reduction with Adaptive Sample-Based Gaussian-Encoded Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16024)
*Michael R. Martin,Joseph Insley,Victor A. Mateevitsi,Silvio Rizzi,Kwan-Liu Ma*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一的基于采样点的固定预算高斯编码方法，能够在单一框架下处理规则网格、非结构网格和粒子数据，并以预设的高斯基元数量和编码大小实现数据压缩。相比先前方法，该方法以更少基元获得更高重建精度，并支持时序数据的温启动加速优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学模拟中的场数据常根据需求采用规则网格（计算效率）、非结构网格（几何/自适应灵活）或粒子格式（追踪运动与形变）表示，但这些格式通常各自使用独立的数据处理管线，缺乏统一的表示与压缩框架。

Method: 作者提出一种统一的基于采样点的高斯编码方法，在固定预算（预先设定的基元数量与编码大小）约束下，直接从输入采样点初始化并优化高斯基元，从而实现可控的数据压缩。对时序数据采用前一时刻结果进行温启动，以减少达到同等重建质量所需的优化开销。

Result: 在结构化、非结构和粒子数据上，基于采样的方法相比先前方法以更少基元提升了重建精度，PSNR 最高提升 4.8 dB，同时基元数量约减少 44 倍。时序数据温启动可在保持独立训练重建质量的同时降低优化成本。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一个统一的固定预算高斯编码框架，适用于结构化、粒子、非结构化及时变科学数据，具有可预测的存储开销、更高的重建精度和更高的时序编码效率。

Abstract: In scientific simulation, regular grids, unstructured meshes, and particle-based formats are chosen to represent field data for computational efficiency, geometry/adaptive flexibility, and following motion/deformation, respectively. Each of these field data formats is often handled through separate data-specific processing pipelines. We present a unified sample-based Gaussian encoding method that represents these data forms under a single fixed-budget formulation. The method initializes and refines Gaussian primitives directly from the input samples while preserving a prescribed primitive count and encoded size to achieve a desired level of data reduction. Across structured, unstructured, and particle data, the sample-based formulation improves reconstruction accuracy with measurably fewer primitives in comparison to prior formulations, achieving up to 4.8 dB higher PSNR with an approximate 44x reduction in primitive count. For time-varying data, warm-starting from the previous timestep reduces the optimization required to reach independently trained reconstruction quality. Together, these results demonstrate a unified fixed-budget Gaussian encoding framework for structured, particle, unstructured, and time-varying scientific data with predictable storage, higher reconstruction accuracy, and improved temporal encoding efficiency.

</details>


### [244] [Non-uniform B-spline optimization method for generating swept surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16042)
*Xiaoyan Kui,Min Yang,Songpeng Yao,Hao Wang,Enya Shen,Qinsong Li,Beiji Zou*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于非均匀B样条的扫掠曲面优化逼近方法，通过分析离散点的几何特征选取特征点，在保证精度的同时显著减少控制点数量并降低逼近误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扫掠曲面构造在计算机辅助设计中应用广泛，但现有方法在逼近精度与控制点数量之间难以兼顾。作者旨在提出一种优化方法，以更少的控制点达到相同或更高的逼近精度，从而提升扫掠曲面的近似质量与效率。

Method: 该方法首先计算扫掠形体上的离散点，并利用表面积、离散曲率、一阶导数及其旋转角等几何属性构建表征曲面不规则程度的分布函数，并根据样本调整权重；随后基于该分布函数选取特征点，通过反算确定近似非均匀B样条曲面的控制点，生成优化逼近结果；最后根据估计的逼近误差自适应调整特征点数量。

Result: 在969个随机生成的扫掠样本和1个管道实例上的实验表明：在0.01的规定精度下，所提算法用更少的控制点达到相近精度，控制点数量平均减少约15.85%；在采样点充足、特征点倍数为路径控制点数10倍时，平均误差降低约51.35%，优于同类方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的非均匀B样条扫掠曲面优化逼近方法能有效减少控制点数量并显著降低逼近误差，在效率和精度上均优于现有同类方法，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: Swept surface construction is widely used in computer-aided design. We propose a novel optimization method using non-uniform B-splines to improve the approximate accuracy of swept surfaces. First, discrete points on the swept shape are computed, and geometric properties such as surface area, discrete curvature, first-order derivatives, and their rotation angles are used to derive a distribution function representing surface irregularity, with weights adjusted from samples. Then, feature points are selected based on the distribution function to determine control points for the approximate non-uniform B-spline surface via inverse calculation, producing an optimized approximation. Finally, the number of feature points is adjusted based on the estimated approximation error. Experiments on 969 randomly generated sweep samples and 1 pipe example show that the proposed algorithm achieves similar accuracy with fewer control points, reducing them by about 15.85% at a specified accuracy of 0.01. Moreover, with ample sampling points, it reduces the average error by approximately 51.35% when the feature point multiple is 10 times the path control points, outperforming comparable methods.

</details>


### [245] [Delayed-Light Rendering for Superluminal Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16180)
*David Bizzozero*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在非相对论场景下以有限信号速度 c 进行实时渲染的方法，能够精确枚举超光速物体产生的多重影像、成对生灭与倒放等效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统渲染通常假设信息瞬时传播，但当成像信号速度有限（且物体可超光速运动）时，会产生多重影像、影像成对创生/湮灭（焦散闪烁）以及时间倒放等复杂视觉现象；现有方法多为近似处理，缺乏对每个采样点都精确的枚举方案，也无法自然地支持任意观测点集合与动画资产的自适应。

Method: 将成像问题转化为对记录的状态历史求解“发射条件”的根：因固定步长模拟产生分段线性历史，每一段的发射条件退化为二次方程，其判别式检测影像对创生，斜率判定每个影像的播放方向、速率与亮度。用向后光锥的上包络构造任意有限观测事件集合的全局延迟景观；引入单调发射钳制保证画面不回退到更旧影像。逐顶点求解以处理跨越延迟梯度的物体剪切；将预生成的帧序列资产视为 (x,y,t) 体积，用求解出的发射曲面切片来自适应地产生变速、倒放与体内去同步效果。方法在平面中阐述，发射条件与逐段求解是范数条件、与维度无关，但 3D 的可见性与遮挡问题不在范围之内。

Result: 该方法已部署于一款已发布的即时战略游戏中，通过针对性优化达到交互帧率，并借助独立参考实现对各算法进行了测量验证；实现对超光速多重影像、成对生灭、时间倒放等效应的逐点精确枚举，且无需动画特定代码即可实现播放速率与体内去同步。

Conclusion: 通过将发射条件化为逐段二次方程的精确求解，本文方法在有限信号速度场景下以实时性能精确再现了超光速物体的复杂感知效应，并已在商业游戏中落地验证其可行性。

Abstract: We present a real-time rendering method for scenes perceived through signals of finite speed $c$ in a non-relativistic setting: $c$ is a property of the imaging signal, not the causal speed limit, so bodies may move faster than $c$. A superluminal body presents several simultaneous images; pairs are created and annihilated in caustic flashes, and some branches play backward in time. Rather than approximating these effects, we enumerate them as roots of an emission condition posed against recorded state history, exact at every point solved, up to one stated approximation for moving observers. Because a fixed-step simulation records piecewise-linear history, the emission condition restricted to one history segment is a quadratic whose discriminant detects image-pair creation and whose slope classifies each image's playback direction, rate, and brightness. Perception is not tied to a single reference point: a global perceived state is built for an arbitrary finite set of observation events, and the delay landscape is the upper envelope of their backward light cones. A monotonic emission clamp guarantees the picture never regresses to images older than those already shown. Per-vertex solves shear bodies straddling delay gradients, and pre-generated frame-sequence assets are adapted by treating them as $(x,y,t)$ volumes sliced by the solved emission surface, so playback rate, reversal, and intra-body de-phasing arise with no animation-specific code. The exposition and implementations are planar: the emission condition and its per-segment solve are norm conditions, independent of dimension, but the visibility and occlusion questions a 3D renderer must answer are out of scope. The method is deployed in a released real-time strategy game; we describe the optimizations that make it run at interactive rates, and measure the algorithms through independent reference implementations.

</details>


### [246] [Dichoptic Foveation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16385)
*Henry Kam,Colin Groth,Jenna Kang,Pratham Saraf,Qi Sun,Kenneth Chen*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文研究双眼（dichoptic）频率差异对自然图像感知的影响，提出"双眼中央凹化（dichoptic foveation）"框架，通过在VR头显中测量不同视网膜离心率下的感知数据，建立4D计算模型，并将其应用于改进中央凹渲染（foveated rendering）系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视觉中双眼间细节差异可提升对比度，且频率差异会导致模糊抑制（blur suppression），融合感知通常由更清晰的图像主导。作者希望利用这一特性量化双眼频率差异在视场各处的感知效应，从而在渲染中减少单眼所需的计算量。

Method: 提出"双眼中央凹化"：对一只眼施加模糊、同时对另一只眼施加锐化滤波，在VR HMD中于不同视网膜离心率呈现自然图像刺激。以心理物理学实验测量感知，将数据映射到JOD（刚可觉差）尺度，并用一个4D计算模型拟合该数据。

Result: 结果表明双眼间频率差异可以用一个简单的4D计算模型很好地描述；该模型量化了不同离心率与频率差异下的感知效应。

Conclusion: 将该模型应用于真实VR场景（自由探索360°视频），通过双眼式增强高频信息改进基础中央凹渲染系统，验证了利用双眼频率差异优化渲染质量的可行性。

Abstract: Interocular differences in visual perception can induce a variety of effects when fused by the brain. For example, prior works have found that carefully crafted binocular differences in local detail can improve contrast. It has also been found that when the frequency content of two stimuli are slightly different, blur suppression leads to a fused percept that is typically dominated by the sharper image. In this paper, we develop a psychophysical framework to measure the perception of natural image stimuli with interocular frequency differences across the visual field. To this end, we study the effect of dichoptic foveation, which we define as the application of blur to one eye and a simultaneous sharpening filter to the other. Stimuli were viewed in a virtual reality (VR) head-mounted display (HMD) and placed at different retinal eccentricities. Study data were scaled to a perceptual just objectionable difference (JOD) scale, and a 4D model was fit to it; our results suggest that interocular frequency differences can be well described by a simple computational model. We applied the model in a realistic VR scenario with free exploration of 360° videos to improve a base foveated rendering system by enhancing high frequency information dichoptically.

</details>


### [247] [HairCS: Reconstructing Strand-Based Hair from Hair Cards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16465)
*Zixuan Lu,Tongtong Wang,Yuefan Shen,Zhongtian Zheng,Chenfanfu Jiang,Yin Yang,Kui Wu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自动化流程，将发卡（hair-card）模型转换为高质量的基于发丝（strand）的发型表示，在保留原始发型的同时补充精细化几何细节并满足制作规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发卡模型在影视/游戏制作中广泛使用，但缺乏发丝级几何细节，无法直接用于基于发丝的渲染、物理模拟和修饰工具；手动将发卡模型转换为发丝模型成本高昂、耗时长，因此需要自动化的转换方法，并满足发丝源于头皮、根部均匀分布、发量合理填充等生产要求。

Method: 给定一组带纹理的三角形或四边形条带（发卡）作为输入，通过自动化流水线生成发丝级表示：保留原始发型轮廓，补充细尺度几何细节，并保证发丝从头皮生长、根部均匀分布、发量（hair volume）合理填充。

Result: 生成的资产可直接用于基于发丝的渲染、物理模拟以及常见修饰工具（如聚束 clumping、卷曲 curling、噪声 noise）。方法在大量且多样的发型上进行验证，包括短发、长发、卷发，以及发髻（buns）、马尾（ponytails）等复杂造型。

Conclusion: 该自动化管线能够高效地将发卡模型转换为高质量、符合生产规范的发丝式发型，兼顾真实感与艺术可控性，显著降低手工转换的工作量，并广泛适用于各类发型。

Abstract: We present an automated pipeline that converts hair-card models into high-quality strand-based hairstyles. Given a collection of textured triangular or quad strips as input, our method produces a strand-based representation that preserves the original hairstyle while enriching it with fine-scale geometric detail and adhering to standard production requirements: strands originate from the scalp, roots are uniformly distributed, and the hair volume is plausibly filled. The resulting assets are directly compatible with strand-based rendering, physics-based simulation, and common grooming modifiers (e.g., clumping, curling, noise) for enhanced realism and artistic control. We validate our approach on a large and diverse set of hairstyles, including short and long hair, curly styles, and complex styles such as buns and ponytails.

</details>
